<main class="wrap stories"><article class="story top" id="story-47259177" data-id="47259177" data-tags="Legal|Open Source|Code" data-score="269" data-comments="168" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47259177" data-time="1772699871">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/chardet/chardet/issues/327" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#64748b' data-tag='Legal'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/chardet/chardet/issues/327" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>No right to relicense this project</span><span class='zh'>无权重新授权此项目</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Discusses licensing rights around relicensing a project, arguing that the author cannot unilaterally relicense without consent or that relicensing rights are contested.</span><span class='zh'>探讨关于对一个项目进行再授权的许可权问题，认为作者在未获得同意的情况下不能单方面改变授权，或指出再授权权利存在争议。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Discusses licensing rights around relicensing a project, arguing that the author cannot unilaterally relicense without consent or that relicensing rights are contested. The post underscores how licensing choices affect community adoption and collaboration.</span><span class='zh'>探讨关于对一个项目进行再授权的许可权问题，认为作者在未获得同意的情况下不能单方面改变授权，或指出再授权权利存在争议。文章强调许可选择会影响社区采纳与协作。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">02</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/03/say-hello-to-macbook-neo/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>MacBook Neo</span><span class='zh'>MacBook Neo 发布</span></a><span class="dom">apple.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple unveils the MacBook Neo in a formal newsroom release, signaling another hardware refresh in its laptop lineup.</span><span class='zh'>苹果在新闻稿中公布 MacBook Neo，标志着其笔记本阵容的又一次硬件升级。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple unveils the MacBook Neo in a formal newsroom release, signaling another hardware refresh in its laptop lineup. The message emphasizes portability and efficiency, inviting comparisons with the current generation and expectations for next-gen Apple silicon and features. It underscores Apple&#x27;s ongoing focus on energy-efficient performance in premium laptops.</span><span class='zh'>苹果在新闻稿中公布 MacBook Neo，标志着其笔记本阵容的又一次硬件升级。文案强调便携性与高效性，并引发对新一代芯片与功能的期待，同时凸显对高端笔记本能源效率的持续追求。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">03</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/googleworkspace/cli" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google Workspace CLI</span><span class='zh'>Google Workspace 命令行工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Google Workspace CLI project provides a command-line interface for automating and scripting Workspace admin tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Google Workspace CLI 提供了用于自动化和脚本化管理任务的命令行界面，方便开发者对用户、配置等执行操作并集成到 CI/CD 流水线。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Google Workspace CLI project provides a command-line interface for automating and scripting Workspace admin tasks. It enables developers to manage users, drive configurations, and integrate with existing CI/CD workflows. The move aligns with a broader push to make cloud admin workflows more programmable.</span><span class='zh'>Google Workspace CLI 提供了用于自动化和脚本化管理任务的命令行界面，方便开发者对用户、配置等执行操作并集成到 CI/CD 流水线。此举体现云端管理工作流向可编程化的趋势，提升运维效率。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic&#x27;s CEO Dario Amodei criticized OpenAI&#x27;s public messaging about a military deal as &#x27;straight up lies&#x27;.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 的首席执行官 Dario Amodei 指出 OpenAI 就军事交易的公开表述是直截了当的谎言。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic&#x27;s CEO Dario Amodei criticized OpenAI&#x27;s public messaging about a military deal as &#x27;straight up lies&#x27;. The comment underscores tension around AI governance, defense partnerships, and corporate communications. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A project aims to build a new Flash-like experience on modern platforms, reviving the classic animation and game ecosystem from the old web.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目旨在在现代平台上打造类似 Flash 的全新体验，以复兴旧网页时代的动画和游戏生态。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A project aims to build a new Flash-like experience on modern platforms, reviving the classic animation and game ecosystem from the old web. It highlights the community&#x27;s desire for open, accessible tooling and the challenges of sustaining a Flash-era ecosystem in today’s tech landscape.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目旨在在现代平台上打造类似 Flash 的全新体验，以复兴旧网页时代的动画和游戏生态。它凸显社区对开放、易获取工具的渴望，以及在当今技术环境中维持 Flash 时代生态所面临的挑战。</span></p>

The project demonstrates how mapping can contextualize safety tech in communities.</span><span class='zh'>一张地图展示了 Flock 摄像头网络的分布与覆盖范围，使公众能够更直观看到用于公共或半公共空间的监控技术。随之而来的，是对隐私的关切，因为摄像头布点等信息更加透明可见。该项目展示了如何通过地图来理解和评估社区中的安防技术。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://terriblesoftware.org/2026/03/03/nobody-gets-promoted-for-simplicity/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nobody gets promoted for simplicity</span><span class='zh'>没人因为简单而升职</span></a><span class="dom">terriblesoftware.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An opinion piece argues that promotions in many tech organizations reward complexity and visibility rather than keeping systems simple.</span><span class='zh'>一篇观点文章认为，许多科技组织的晋升机制偏向复杂性和可见度，而非坚持简单设计。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An opinion piece argues that promotions in many tech organizations reward complexity and visibility rather than keeping systems simple. The author points to entrenched incentives that favor adding features, optimization, or risk-taking over clean, minimal design. The takeaway is to prioritize clarity, reliability, and lean architectures to unlock long-term value and career advancement.</span><span class='zh'>一篇观点文章认为，许多科技组织的晋升机制偏向复杂性和可见度，而非坚持简单设计。作者指出习以成年的激励让人们更重视增添功能、优化和冒险，而不是追求清晰、稳定的系统。核心信息是，长远价值来自清晰、可靠、简化的架构，有助于职业发展的提升。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://grapheneos.social/@GrapheneOS/116160393783585567" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Motorola GrapheneOS devices will be bootloader unlockable/relockable</span><span class='zh'>摩托罗拉 GrapheneOS 设备将实现引导加载程序可解锁/重新锁定</span></a><span class="dom">grapheneos.social</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GrapheneOS will allow bootloader unlock and relock on Motorola devices, signaling a shift in how open-source security projects balance developer flexibility with device security.</span><span class='zh'>GrapheneOS 将在摩托罗拉设备上实现引导加载程序的解锁与重新锁定。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GrapheneOS will allow bootloader unlock and relock on Motorola devices, signaling a shift in how open-source security projects balance developer flexibility with device security. This could ease custom ROM flashing and development while raising questions about root access, factory resets, and secure update chains.</span><span class='zh'>GrapheneOS 将在摩托罗拉设备上实现引导加载程序的解锁与重新锁定。这为开发者提供更大灵活性，同时也引发关于 root 权限、出厂重置与安全更新链的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.experimental-history.com/p/the-one-science-reform-we-can-all" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Government grant-funded research should not be published in for-profit journals</span><span class='zh'>政府资助研究不应在营利性期刊发表</span></a><span class="dom">experimental-history.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A column argues that publicly funded science should be published in non-profit or open venues rather than for-profit journals.</span><span class='zh'>一篇专栏认为政府资助的科研应在非营利性或开放渠道发表，而非营利性期刊。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A column argues that publicly funded science should be published in non-profit or open venues rather than for-profit journals. It discusses how paywalls and profit motives can distort access and research dissemination. This stance aligns with ongoing debates about scientific publishing reform.</span><span class='zh'>一篇专栏认为政府资助的科研应在非营利性或开放渠道发表，而非营利性期刊。文章指出付费墙与利润动机可能扭曲信息获取与研究传播，与当前关于科学出版改革的讨论相呼应。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google is dropping the 30% Play Store fee and signaling openness to third-party app stores, potentially reshaping Android app distribution and competition.</span><span class='zh'>Google 取消 Play Store 的 30% 提成并表示欢迎第三方应用商店，这可能重新塑造 Android 应用分发与竞争格局。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google is dropping the 30% Play Store fee and signaling openness to third-party app stores, potentially reshaping Android app distribution and competition. The move could boost developer choice while raising questions about security and ecosystem fragmentation.</span><span class='zh'>Google 取消 Play Store 的 30% 提成并表示欢迎第三方应用商店，这可能重新塑造 Android 应用分发与竞争格局。此举或提升开发者选择，同时也引发对安全性和生态系统碎片化的担忧。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://neilzone.co.uk/2026/03/im-struggling-to-think-of-any-online-services-for-which-id-be-willing-to-verify-my-identity-or-age/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I&#x27;m reluctant to verify my identity or age for any online services</span><span class='zh'>我不愿意在任何在线服务上验证身份或年龄</span></a><span class="dom">neilzone.co.uk</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Verifying identity or age has become a friction point in many online services, and I’m reluctant to do it.</span><span class='zh'>在线服务对身份/年龄的验证日益增多，造成用户体验负担。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Verifying identity or age has become a friction point in many online services, and I’m reluctant to do it. The post argues that invasive KYC checks erode privacy and push users away, and it hints at privacy-preserving alternatives and the broader trend toward minimal verification.</span><span class='zh'>在线服务对身份/年龄的验证日益增多，造成用户体验负担。该文指出强制性的身份核验侵蚀隐私、抑制使用意愿，并探讨更隐私友好的身份认证替代方案以及数字身份的未来趋势。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cly2m5e5ke4o" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>TikTok will not introduce end-to-end encryption, saying it makes users less safe</span><span class='zh'>TikTok 不引入端到端加密，称这会让用户更不安全</span></a><span class="dom">bbc.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>TikTok states it will not implement end-to-end encryption, arguing it could endanger users or complicate moderation, while privacy advocates push for stronger encryption.</span><span class='zh'>TikTok 表示不采用端对端加密，理由是可能危及用户安全或让内容审核更复杂。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>TikTok states it will not implement end-to-end encryption, arguing it could endanger users or complicate moderation, while privacy advocates push for stronger encryption. The stance underscores the ongoing tension between user safety, regulatory compliance, and privacy on popular social apps.</span><span class='zh'>TikTok 表示不采用端对端加密，理由是可能危及用户安全或让内容审核更复杂。此立场凸显在流行社交应用中，隐私、合规与安全之间的持续博弈。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc9849.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>RFC 9849. TLS Encrypted Client Hello</span><span class='zh'>TLS Encrypted Client Hello（RFC 9849）</span></a><span class="dom">rfc-editor.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Explains RFC 9849, which encrypts the TLS ClientHello to protect metadata during handshake from passive observers.</span><span class='zh'>介绍 RFC 9849，它在 TLS 握手阶段对 ClientHello 进行加密，以保护元数据不被被动监听者获取。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Explains RFC 9849, which encrypts the TLS ClientHello to protect metadata during handshake from passive observers. It discusses deployment considerations, compatibility, and potential performance impacts.</span><span class='zh'>介绍 RFC 9849，它在 TLS 握手阶段对 ClientHello 进行加密，以保护元数据不被被动监听者获取。讨论部署注意事项、兼容性以及潜在的性能影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A physics explainer video on how the Super-Kamiokande detector images the Sun by detecting neutrinos, illustrating how particle physics probes solar processes. The video translates complex detector science into intuition about neutrino astronomy and why it matters for understanding fusion in the Sun.</span><span class='zh'>该视频讲解通过探测中微子来成像太阳，展示大规模探测器如何揭示太阳的核聚变过程。内容将高能物理与天文学交汇，帮助理解中微子天文学的重要性。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A prominent open-source maintainer laments SEO challenges and discovers that organic search visibility can dwarf good code. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/robertcprice/nCPU" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A CPU that runs entirely on GPU</span><span class='zh'>完全在 GPU 上运行的 CPU</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Describes a software-based CPU that executes on GPU hardware, exploring potential performance advantages for parallel workloads and the challenges of mapping CPU semantics to GPU architectures.</span><span class='zh'>描述一个在 GPU 硬件上执行的 CPU 架构，探讨并行工作负载下的潜在性能优势以及将 CPU 语义映射到 GPU 架构所面临的挑战，呈现硬件-软件协同设计的最新思路。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Describes a software-based CPU that executes on GPU hardware, exploring potential performance advantages for parallel workloads and the challenges of mapping CPU semantics to GPU architectures. It highlights evolving ideas about hardware-software co-design.</span><span class='zh'>描述一个在 GPU 硬件上执行的 CPU 架构，探讨并行工作负载下的潜在性能优势以及将 CPU 语义映射到 GPU 架构所面临的挑战，呈现硬件-软件协同设计的最新思路。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://terriblesoftware.org/2026/03/03/nobody-gets-promoted-for-simplicity/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nobody gets promoted for simplicity</span><span class='zh'>没人因为简单而升职</span></a><span class="dom">terriblesoftware.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Another take on the same thesis, elaborating how teams often equate simplicity with risk, and why leadership should reward clean, maintainable systems for sustainable progress.</span><span class='zh'>对同一论题的另一种解读，阐述团队常把简单等同于风险，强调领导层应当奖励干净、可维护的系统，以实现可持续的进展。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Another take on the same thesis, elaborating how teams often equate simplicity with risk, and why leadership should reward clean, maintainable systems for sustainable progress.</span><span class='zh'>对同一论题的另一种解读，阐述团队常把简单等同于风险，强调领导层应当奖励干净、可维护的系统，以实现可持续的进展。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://leodemoura.github.io/blog/2026/02/28/when-ai-writes-the-worlds-software.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>When AI writes the software, who verifies it?</span><span class='zh'>当 AI 编写软件，谁来验证它？</span></a><span class="dom">leodemoura.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues verification remains the hard part when AI writes software; human audits, formal methods, and reliable pipelines are needed to ensure correctness, security, and compliance.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为当 AI 编写软件时，验证仍是最难的环节，需要人工审查、形式化方法与可靠的开发流程来确保正确性、安 全与合规，并讨论治理与对 AI 产出的过度信任风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues verification remains the hard part when AI writes software; human audits, formal methods, and reliable pipelines are needed to ensure correctness, security, and compliance. It discusses governance and the risk of relying solely on AI for critical code.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为当 AI 编写软件时，验证仍是最难的环节，需要人工审查、形式化方法与可靠的开发流程来确保正确性、安 全与合规，并讨论治理与对 AI 产出的过度信任风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.wired.com/story/coruna-iphone-hacking-toolkit-us-government/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Possible US Government iPhone-Hacking Toolkit in foreign spy and criminal hands</span><span class='zh'>美国政府 iPhone 黑客工具包可能落入外国间谍与犯罪分子之手</span></a><span class="dom">wired.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Wired reports on a coercive cyber toolkit used by government intelligence that could fall into foreign spies or criminals&#x27; hands, raising concerns about supply chain, weaponization of iOS exploits, and export controls.</span><span class='zh'>Wired 报道称一款来自政府情报部门的网络工具包可能流入外国间谍与犯罪分子之手，引发对供应链、iOS 漏洞的武器化及出口管制的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Wired reports on a coercive cyber toolkit used by government intelligence that could fall into foreign spies or criminals&#x27; hands, raising concerns about supply chain, weaponization of iOS exploits, and export controls. The piece warns of consequences for user privacy and national security.</span><span class='zh'>Wired 报道称一款来自政府情报部门的网络工具包可能流入外国间谍与犯罪分子之手，引发对供应链、iOS 漏洞的武器化及出口管制的担忧。此事对用户隐私与国家安全的潜在影响需关注。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://growtika.com/blog/tech-media-collapse" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Most-read tech publications have lost over half their Google traffic since 2024</span><span class='zh'>自 2024 年以来，大多数科技出版物的 Google 流量下降超过一半</span></a><span class="dom">growtika.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Tech publications have lost over half of their Google traffic since 2024, signaling a seismic shift in how audiences discover content and the pressure on publishers to diversify referrals, subscription models, and direct visits.</span><span class='zh'>自 2024 年以来，主流科技媒体的 Google 流量下降超过一半，标志着受众发现内容方式的重大转变，也给出版商带来多元化流量来源、订阅模式和直接访问的压力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Tech publications have lost over half of their Google traffic since 2024, signaling a seismic shift in how audiences discover content and the pressure on publishers to diversify referrals, subscription models, and direct visits. The trend underscores the importance of strong brand signals, newsletters, and alternative distribution channels.</span><span class='zh'>自 2024 年以来，主流科技媒体的 Google 流量下降超过一半，标志着受众发现内容方式的重大转变，也给出版商带来多元化流量来源、订阅模式和直接访问的压力。此趋势强调强化品牌信号、邮件通讯与其他分发渠道的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://tonsky.me/blog/fall-of-native/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude is an Electron App because we&#x27;ve lost native</span><span class='zh'>Claude 是 Electron 应用，因为我们已经失去了原生</span></a><span class="dom">tonsky.me</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Claude UI reliance on Electron attributed to lack of native UI across platforms; the article argues about dwindling native UI support and the trade-offs of cross-platform web tech.</span><span class='zh'>文中将 Claude 的桌面客户端归因于 Electron，认为跨平台原生 UI 支持的减少使人们转向 Web 技术。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Claude UI reliance on Electron attributed to lack of native UI across platforms; the article argues about dwindling native UI support and the trade-offs of cross-platform web tech. It discusses performance and UX costs of Electron apps.</span><span class='zh'>文中将 Claude 的桌面客户端归因于 Electron，认为跨平台原生 UI 支持的减少使人们转向 Web 技术。讨论 Electron 应用对性能与用户体验的权衡，以及原生 UI 的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theverge.com/policy/887678/supreme-court-ai-art-copyright" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI-generated art can’t be copyrighted after Supreme Court declines review</span><span class='zh'>AI 生成的艺术在最高法院拒绝复审后不能获得版权</span></a><span class="dom">theverge.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Supreme Court declines to review whether AI-generated art can be copyrighted, effectively leaving the rule that AI-created works cannot be copyrighted in place.</span><span class='zh'>最高法院拒绝就 AI 生成艺术的版权问题再审，维持现行规则：AI 创作的作品通常不能获得版权保护。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Supreme Court declines to review whether AI-generated art can be copyrighted, effectively leaving the rule that AI-created works cannot be copyrighted in place. This decision shapes IP strategy for artists and platforms navigating AI-assisted workflows.</span><span class='zh'>最高法院拒绝就 AI 生成艺术的版权问题再审，维持现行规则：AI 创作的作品通常不能获得版权保护。这对艺术家与在 AI 辅助创作中运作的平台的知识产权策略具有重要影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://techcrunch.com/2026/03/04/jensen-huang-says-nvidia-is-pulling-back-from-openai-and-anthropic-but-his-explanation-raises-more-questions-than-it-answers/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Jensen Huang says Nvidia is pulling back from OpenAI and Anthropic</span><span class='zh'>黄仁勋称 Nvidia 正在从 OpenAI 与 Anthropic 收缩</span></a><span class="dom">techcrunch.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Jensen Huang says Nvidia is pulling back from OpenAI and Anthropic, but his rationale raises more questions than it answers.</span><span class='zh'>黄仁勋表示英伟达正在从 OpenAI 与 Anthropic 的合作中撤出，但其解释引发更多疑问。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Jensen Huang says Nvidia is pulling back from OpenAI and Anthropic, but his rationale raises more questions than it answers. The remark hints at shifting strategic alignments for Nvidia as a compute provider in AI, with potential implications for partnerships and access to hardware.</span><span class='zh'>黄仁勋表示英伟达正在从 OpenAI 与 Anthropic 的合作中撤出，但其解释引发更多疑问。此举折射出算力提供商在 AI 生态中的角色变化，以及对伙伴关系与硬件获取的潜在影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://poppy-connection-keeper.netlify.app/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Poppy – a simple app to stay intentional with relationships</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Poppy——一款帮助在关系中保持有意识的简单应用</span></a><span class="dom">poppy-connection-keeper.netlify.app</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Poppy is a minimal app designed to help people nurture relationships by keeping goals and meaningful interactions in view.</span><span class='zh'>Poppy 是一个极简应用，帮助用户通过记录重要互动和设定关系目标来经营人际关系。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Poppy is a minimal app designed to help people nurture relationships by keeping goals and meaningful interactions in view. It demonstrates how a focused, single-purpose tool can outperform bloated social apps for personal care. The project also reminds us that thoughtful UX can outperform feature-heavy options in the relationship space.</span><span class='zh'>Poppy 是一个极简应用，帮助用户通过记录重要互动和设定关系目标来经营人际关系。它展示了一个小而聚焦的工具如何在拥挤的应用生态中帮助人们更有意识地行动。该项目也提醒人们，深思熟虑的用户体验往往胜过功能繁多的社交应用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.poetryfoundation.org/poems/46560/dulce-et-decorum-est" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Dulce et Decorum Est (1921)</span><span class='zh'>《甜蜜与荣光的陨落》（1921）</span></a><span class="dom">poetryfoundation.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Dulce et Decorum Est exposes the horrors of war and mocks patriotic rhetoric that praises dying for one’s country.</span><span class='zh'>这首诗揭示战争的惨烈以及对为国捐躯的爱国言辞的讽刺。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Dulce et Decorum Est exposes the horrors of war and mocks patriotic rhetoric that praises dying for one’s country. Owen uses vivid, grim imagery to shock readers into questioning the cost of conflict.</span><span class='zh'>这首诗揭示战争的惨烈以及对为国捐躯的爱国言辞的讽刺。欧文通过生动而冷峻的意象震撼读者，促使人们质疑冲突的代价。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.inngest.com/blog/no-lost-updates-python-asyncio" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>What Python&#x27;s asyncio primitives get wrong about shared state</span><span class='zh'>Python 的 asyncio 原语在共享状态方面哪里错了</span></a><span class="dom">inngest.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Python&#x27;s asyncio primitives can mishandle shared state, leading to subtle race conditions or lost updates in concurrent code.</span><span class='zh'>Python 的 asyncio 原语在处理共享状态时容易出错，可能在并发代码中出现细微的竞争条件或丢失更新。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Python&#x27;s asyncio primitives can mishandle shared state, leading to subtle race conditions or lost updates in concurrent code. The piece surveys patterns that avoid these pitfalls, such as explicit synchronization, avoiding shared mutable state, and using higher-level abstractions.</span><span class='zh'>Python 的 asyncio 原语在处理共享状态时容易出错，可能在并发代码中出现细微的竞争条件或丢失更新。文章探讨了避免这些坑的模式，如显式同步、避免共享可变状态，以及使用更高层的抽象。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://qlabs.sh/slowrun" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>NanoGPT Slowrun: Language Modeling with Limited Data, Infinite Compute</span><span class='zh'>NanoGPT Slowrun：在有限数据、无限计算的语言建模</span></a><span class="dom">qlabs.sh</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Slowrun explores language modeling with limited data but abundant compute, using strategies that maximize training efficiency and data augmentation.</span><span class='zh'>Slowrun 探索在数据有限、计算资源充足的条件下进行语言建模，利用高效训练与数据增广等策略。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Slowrun explores language modeling with limited data but abundant compute, using strategies that maximize training efficiency and data augmentation. It demonstrates that clever data regimes and scaling can yield competitive models without huge datasets.</span><span class='zh'>Slowrun 探索在数据有限、计算资源充足的条件下进行语言建模，利用高效训练与数据增广等策略。显示出通过巧妙的数据方案与扩展规模，即使数据量不大也能训练出具有竞争力的模型。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/mar/04/us-tech-companies-energy-cost-pledge-white-house" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>US tech firms pledge at White House to bear costs of energy for datacenters</span><span class='zh'>美国科技公司承诺在白宫承担数据中心能源成本</span></a><span class="dom">theguardian.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Tech companies pledge at the White House to bear energy costs for data centers, signaling policy-driven pressure to manage rising energy bills.</span><span class='zh'>科技公司在白宫承诺承担数据中心能源成本，显示出为应对日益上升的能源账单与电网压力而推动的政策导向。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Tech companies pledge at the White House to bear energy costs for data centers, signaling policy-driven pressure to manage rising energy bills. The move could influence datacenter location decisions and spur investments in energy efficiency and grid resilience.</span><span class='zh'>科技公司在白宫承诺承担数据中心能源成本，显示出为应对日益上升的能源账单与电网压力而推动的政策导向。此举可能影响数据中心选址与能效投资，并体现政企在能源议题上的协同。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.carolinecrampton.com/the-view-from-rss/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The View from RSS</span><span class='zh'>来自 RSS 的视角</span></a><span class="dom">carolinecrampton.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This piece defends RSS as a resilient, privacy-preserving way to consume content in an era of algorithm-driven feeds.</span><span class='zh'>本文捍卫 RSS 作为在算法驱动的信息流时代仍具韧性、重视隐私的内容获取方式。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This piece defends RSS as a resilient, privacy-preserving way to consume content in an era of algorithm-driven feeds. It discusses open standards, the fragility of platform lock-in, and why independent feeds still matter for creators and readers.</span><span class='zh'>本文捍卫 RSS 作为在算法驱动的信息流时代仍具韧性、重视隐私的内容获取方式。讨论开放标准、平台锁定的脆弱性，以及独立订阅源对创作者和读者的重要性。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A regulator has contacted Meta regarding workers viewing intimate footage captured by AI glasses.</span><span class='zh'>监管机构就员工使用由 AI 眼镜记录的亲密视频向 Meta 联系，凸显对隐私与监控在 AI 工作环境中的日益关注。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A regulator has contacted Meta regarding workers viewing intimate footage captured by AI glasses. The move highlights growing privacy and surveillance concerns in AI-enabled workplaces, and could spur new guidelines or enforcement actions. Meta faces heightened regulatory scrutiny as these issues come to light.</span><span class='zh'>监管机构就员工使用由 AI 眼镜记录的亲密视频向 Meta 联系，凸显对隐私与监控在 AI 工作环境中的日益关注。此举可能推动新规或执法行动，Meta 将在日益严格的合规环境中面对更多审查。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Examines whether Windows 1.0’s lack of overlapping windows was born from hardware/memory constraints or licensing quirks. The discussion sheds light on how early design choices were shaped more by tech limits than by legality, with implications for UI history.</span><span class='zh'>考察 Windows 1.0 缺少重叠窗口究竟是由硬件/内存限制还是许可问题驱动。讨论揭示早期设计选择更多受技术约束影响而非法律因素，对用户界面史有一定启示。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www-cs-faculty.stanford.edu/~knuth/papers/claude-cycles.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#10b981' data-tag='Science'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=www-cs-faculty.stanford.edu&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>www-cs-faculty.stanford.edu</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www-cs-faculty.stanford.edu/~knuth/papers/claude-cycles.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude&#x27;s Cycles [pdf]</span><span class='zh'>Claude 的循环（PDF）</span></a><span class="dom">www-cs-faculty.stanford.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Stanford/Knuth-hosted paper examines cycles in Claude, offering formal analysis and possibly algorithmic insights.</span><span class='zh'>这份由斯坦福/ Knuth 团队整理的论文探讨了 Claude 中的循环现象，给出形式化分析与潜在的算法洞见。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Stanford/Knuth-hosted paper examines cycles in Claude, offering formal analysis and possibly algorithmic insights. It presents a theoretical exploration of repetitive structures in Claude’s context, with formal proofs or demonstrations. The work contributes to understanding computational cycles and their implications in Claude’s framework.</span><span class='zh'>这份由斯坦福/ Knuth 团队整理的论文探讨了 Claude 中的循环现象，给出形式化分析与潜在的算法洞见。内容聚焦 Claude 语境下的重复结构，包含证明或演示。该研究有助于理解计算循环及其在 Claude 框架中的意义。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://retractionwatch.com/2026/03/03/canadian-pediatric-society-journal-correction-case-reports-fictional-paediatrics-child-health/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Medical journal says the case reports it has published for 25 years are fiction</span><span class='zh'>医学期刊称其过去25年的病例报道为虚构</span></a><span class="dom">retractionwatch.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A medical journal reveals that decades of published case reports are fictional, shaking trust in a long-running publication and raising questions about peer review and data provenance.</span><span class='zh'>一家医学期刊宣布其长期刊载的病例报道其实为虚构，这动摇了长期刊物的公信力，并引发对同行评审与数据可追溯性的质疑。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A medical journal reveals that decades of published case reports are fictional, shaking trust in a long-running publication and raising questions about peer review and data provenance. The episode spotlights ongoing needs for verifiable data and rigorous editorial practices.</span><span class='zh'>一家医学期刊宣布其长期刊载的病例报道其实为虚构，这动摇了长期刊物的公信力，并引发对同行评审与数据可追溯性的质疑。事件凸显对可验证数据与严格编辑实践的持续需求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.cbc.ca/news/canada/british-columbia/b-c-adopting-year-round-daylight-time-9.7111657" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>British Columbia is permanently adopting daylight time</span><span class='zh'>不列颠哥伦比亚省永久采用夏令时</span></a><span class="dom">cbc.ca</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>British Columbia will permanently observe daylight time, ending seasonal clock changes.</span><span class='zh'>不列颠哥伦比亚省将永久采用夏令时，取消季节性时钟调整。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>British Columbia will permanently observe daylight time, ending seasonal clock changes. The move aims to simplify scheduling and energy planning, but could complicate activities tied to daylight patterns and cross-border coordination with the US and other provinces.</span><span class='zh'>不列颠哥伦比亚省将永久采用夏令时，取消季节性时钟调整。此举旨在简化日程与能源规划，但可能让依赖日照模式的活动和跨境协调变得更复杂。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.pnas.org/doi/10.1073/pnas.2520385123" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Humans 40k yrs ago developed a system of conventional signs</span><span class='zh'>四万年前人类发展出一种常规符号体系</span></a><span class="dom">pnas.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PNAS reports that humans 40,000 years ago developed a conventional system of signs, pushing back estimates for symbolic communication.</span><span class='zh'>PNAS 报告称，约 4 万年前的人类已经发展出一套常规符号体系，将符号沟通的时间推前。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PNAS reports that humans 40,000 years ago developed a conventional system of signs, pushing back estimates for symbolic communication. This finding deepens our understanding of cognitive evolution and how early humans represented ideas beyond gesture and ritual.</span><span class='zh'>PNAS 报告称，约 4 万年前的人类已经发展出一套常规符号体系，将符号沟通的时间推前。此发现深化了对认知进化和早期人类如何以符号表达思想的理解。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Shows how dynamic feature detection can enable faster C code by selecting optimized paths at run time, trading a bit of complexity for performance gains on diverse hardware. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://justin.poehnelt.com/posts/rewrite-your-cli-for-ai-agents/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>You need to rewrite your CLI for AI agents</span><span class='zh'>你需要为 AI 代理重写你的命令行界面</span></a><span class="dom">justin.poehnelt.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Traditional CLIs often undercut the capabilities of AI agents due to rigid command structures.</span><span class='zh'>传统的命令行界面对 AI 代理的能力造成限制，往往因结构僵硬而难以支撑多步任务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Traditional CLIs often undercut the capabilities of AI agents due to rigid command structures. The post advocates rewriting a CLI to support state, orchestration, and structured prompts so AI agents can operate more reliably across multi-step tasks. It argues a modern CLI should separate interface from model, enabling better testing and composability.</span><span class='zh'>传统的命令行界面对 AI 代理的能力造成限制，往往因结构僵硬而难以支撑多步任务。文章主张重写 CLI，加入状态管理、编排和结构化提示，使 AI 代理在多步任务中更可靠地工作。它强调将界面与模型解耦以提升可测试性与组合性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/vladich/pg_jitter" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Better JIT for Postgres</span><span class='zh'>PostgreSQL 的更好 JIT</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Pg_jitter proposes a faster JIT path for PostgreSQL, potentially boosting query speed on workloads with many generated queries.</span><span class='zh'>pg_jitter 提出在 PostgreSQL 上实现更快的 JIT 路径，可能提升对高并发和生成型查询的执行速度。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Pg_jitter proposes a faster JIT path for PostgreSQL, potentially boosting query speed on workloads with many generated queries. It highlights the ongoing tension between runtime code generation and maintainability, plus how JIT affects CPU cache and startup cost.</span><span class='zh'>pg_jitter 提出在 PostgreSQL 上实现更快的 JIT 路径，可能提升对高并发和生成型查询的执行速度。揭示了运行时代码生成与可维护性之间的平衡，以及 JIT 对 CPU 缓存和启动成本的影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Ataraxy-Labs/weave" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Weave – A language aware merge algorithm based on entities</span><span class='zh'>Weave——一种基于实体的语言感知合并算法</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Weave is a language-aware merge algorithm that uses entities to resolve conflicts during merges, prioritizing semantics over raw text.</span><span class='zh'>Weave 是一种基于实体的语言感知合并算法，在合并冲突时以实体语义为导向来解决分歧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Weave is a language-aware merge algorithm that uses entities to resolve conflicts during merges, prioritizing semantics over raw text. By aligning changes to underlying entities, it can reduce churn in collaborative editing and improve consistency across versions. The project could influence future diff/merge tooling for structured data.</span><span class='zh'>Weave 是一种基于实体的语言感知合并算法，在合并冲突时以实体语义为导向来解决分歧。通过将改动对齐到实体之上，能够降低协同编辑中的重复冲突并提升版本一致性。该项目可能影响未来对结构化数据的差异和合并工具。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nytimes.com/2026/03/04/science/chimpanzees-crystals.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>To understand our fascination with crystals, researchers gave some to chimps</span><span class='zh'>为理解晶体的迷恋，研究人员给黑猩猩晶体</span></a><span class="dom">nytimes.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A study gave crystals to chimpanzees to probe their fascination with them, offering insights into cognition and cross-species curiosity.</span><span class='zh'>研究将晶体给黑猩猩，以探究它们对晶体的迷恋，揭示认知与跨物种好奇心的线索。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A study gave crystals to chimpanzees to probe their fascination with them, offering insights into cognition and cross-species curiosity. Results suggest that nonhuman primates share at least some aesthetic interest in shiny objects, informing how we study perception.</span><span class='zh'>研究将晶体给黑猩猩，以探究它们对晶体的迷恋，揭示认知与跨物种好奇心的线索。结果表明非人灵长类确实对闪亮物体表现出某种美感兴趣，这有助于理解感知研究。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mas.to/@gabrielesvelto/116171750653898304" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>10% of Firefox crashes are caused by bitflips</span><span class='zh'>Firefox 崩溃中有 10% 由位翻转引发</span></a><span class="dom">mas.to</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Reports that about 10% of Firefox crashes are caused by bitflips in memory, underscoring the impact of hardware faults on software reliability.</span><span class='zh'>报道显示约 10% 的 Firefox 崩溃由内存位翻转引起，凸显硬件故障对软件可靠性的影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Reports that about 10% of Firefox crashes are caused by bitflips in memory, underscoring the impact of hardware faults on software reliability. The finding motivates better error handling, ECC memory use, and robust testing for resilience.</span><span class='zh'>报道显示约 10% 的 Firefox 崩溃由内存位翻转引起，凸显硬件故障对软件可靠性的影响。该发现促使加强错误处理、使用 ECC 内存以及提高容错测试。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mass user churn around ChatGPT signals shifting user sentiment, pricing, or feature tradeoffs, prompting questions about retention and competition in consumer AI. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Daemon (2006) is a Daniel Suarez techno-thriller about a neural-age digital &#x27;daemon&#x27; triggering a cascade of real-world events after a master hacker&#x27;s scheme.</span><span class='zh'>《Daemon》（2006）是丹尼尔·苏雷斯的科技惊悚小说，描述黑客触发的数字“daemon”如何引发现实世界的一系列事件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Daemon (2006) is a Daniel Suarez techno-thriller about a neural-age digital &#x27;daemon&#x27; triggering a cascade of real-world events after a master hacker&#x27;s scheme. The book explores how software-driven systems can shape society and raises questions about control and security.</span><span class='zh'>《Daemon》（2006）是丹尼尔·苏雷斯的科技惊悚小说，描述黑客触发的数字“daemon”如何引发现实世界的一系列事件。作品探讨软件驱动的系统如何影响社会，并提出关于控制与安全的深刻问题。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://jakelazaroff.com/words/an-interactive-intro-to-crdts/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>An Interactive Intro to CRDTs (2023)</span><span class='zh'>CRDT 的互动入门（2023）</span></a><span class="dom">jakelazaroff.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A hands-on, interactive primer on conflict-free replicated data types that lets you experiment with how CRDTs converge across distributed replicas.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个互动式的CRDT入门，允许你通过实验观察分布式副本如何最终一致收敛。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A hands-on, interactive primer on conflict-free replicated data types that lets you experiment with how CRDTs converge across distributed replicas. It covers core ideas, common designs, and practical trade-offs for real-time collaboration and offline-first applications.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个互动式的CRDT入门，允许你通过实验观察分布式副本如何最终一致收敛。内容覆盖核心概念、常见设计及在实时协作和离线优先场景中的权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03251" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Speculative Speculative Decoding (SSD)</span><span class='zh'>推测性解码（SSD）</span></a><span class="dom">arxiv.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Speculative Decoding speeds up autoregressive generation by using a fast draft decoder to propose tokens, with a larger model later verifying and finalizing them.</span><span class='zh'>本文提出的推测性解码策略通过一个快速的草拟解码器先给出候选标记，再由更大模型进行验证和最终确认，以降低推理成本并尽量保持输出质量。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Speculative Decoding speeds up autoregressive generation by using a fast draft decoder to propose tokens, with a larger model later verifying and finalizing them. The extended approach improves throughput while preserving quality, clarifying deployment considerations and trade-offs.</span><span class='zh'>本文提出的推测性解码策略通过一个快速的草拟解码器先给出候选标记，再由更大模型进行验证和最终确认，以降低推理成本并尽量保持输出质量。扩展版本在吞吐量和质量之间权衡，讨论了在实际部署中的应用要点和潜在挑战。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An engineering note detailing techniques used to accelerate regex matching in F#, likely including automata optimization, caching, and compile-time improvements.</span><span class='zh'>一份工程笔记介绍在 F# 中加速正则匹配所采用的技术，如自动机优化、多模式匹配和编译时改进等。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Argues that personality traits in LLMs can improve user trust and productivity, if designed with guardrails.</span><span class='zh'>认为赋予大语言模型个性化特征有助于提升用户信任与效率，前提是设有合适的护栏与规范。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/lixiasky-back/coroTracer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I made a zero-copy coroutine tracer to find my scheduler&#x27;s lost wakeups</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：我做了一个零拷贝的协程跟踪器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>I built a zero-copy coroutine tracer to detect missed wakeups in my scheduler, reducing the overhead of tracing while diagnosing latency issues.</span><span class='zh'>我做了一个零拷贝的协程跟踪器，用来定位调度器中丢失的唤醒，从而在不增加额外开销的情况下诊断延迟问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>I built a zero-copy coroutine tracer to detect missed wakeups in my scheduler, reducing the overhead of tracing while diagnosing latency issues. The approach helps pinpoint wakeup gaps without perturbing the system&#x27;s timing.</span><span class='zh'>我做了一个零拷贝的协程跟踪器，用来定位调度器中丢失的唤醒，从而在不增加额外开销的情况下诊断延迟问题。该方法在不打扰系统时序的前提下找到唤醒间隙。</span></p>

It discusses practical approaches to cleanup and the motivation needed to sustain such civic efforts.</span><span class='zh'>本文讨论在日常生活中捡拾大量垃圾的行动，以及持续进行的小规模清洁如何改善社区与环境。提出切实可行的清洁策略，并强调公民参与的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://lemire.me/blog/2026/02/28/you-can-use-newline-characters-in-urls/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>You can use newline characters in URLs</span><span class='zh'>URL 中可包含换行字符</span></a><span class="dom">lemire.me</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Newline characters can be included in URLs, per recent exploration of encoding and normalization rules.</span><span class='zh'>URL 可以包含换行符，这一发现引发对 URL 处理、安全性（如头注入与日志解析）以及浏览器/服务器对输入进行清洗的策略的讨论。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Newline characters can be included in URLs, per recent exploration of encoding and normalization rules. This opens questions about URL handling, security pitfalls (like header injection or log parsing), and how browsers and servers sanitize input.</span><span class='zh'>URL 可以包含换行符，这一发现引发对 URL 处理、安全性（如头注入与日志解析）以及浏览器/服务器对输入进行清洗的策略的讨论。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">101</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://leandojo.org/torchlean.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>TorchLean: Formalizing Neural Networks in Lean</span><span class='zh'>TorchLean：在 Lean 中形式化神经网络</span></a><span class="dom">leandojo.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Introduces TorchLean, a project to formalize neural networks within the Lean theorem prover, enabling machine-checked proofs of ML models and properties.</span><span class='zh'>介绍 TorchLean，将神经网络形式化到 Lean 定理证明器中，使机器可校验的模型性质成为可能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Introduces TorchLean, a project to formalize neural networks within the Lean theorem prover, enabling machine-checked proofs of ML models and properties. It showcases bridging heavy math with practical ML workflows, potentially increasing reliability and enabling formal verification for critical systems.</span><span class='zh'>介绍 TorchLean，将神经网络形式化到 Lean 定理证明器中，使机器可校验的模型性质成为可能。该工作将高等数学与实际机器学习工作流连接起来，提升可靠性，并为关键系统的形式化验证提供途径。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://play.elevatorsaga.com/index.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Elevator Saga: The elevator programming game (2015)</span><span class='zh'>Elevator Saga：电梯编程游戏（2015）</span></a><span class="dom">play.elevatorsaga.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This browser game challenges players to optimize elevator scheduling under constraints, teaching concepts like concurrency, event-driven design, and systems thinking.</span><span class='zh'>这款浏览器游戏要求玩家在约束条件下优化电梯调度，涵盖并发、事件驱动设计与系统思维等概念。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This browser game challenges players to optimize elevator scheduling under constraints, teaching concepts like concurrency, event-driven design, and systems thinking. It&#x27;s a surprisingly effective sandbox for engineers to practice algorithmic thinking.</span><span class='zh'>这款浏览器游戏要求玩家在约束条件下优化电梯调度，涵盖并发、事件驱动设计与系统思维等概念。它意外成为训练工程师算法思考的高效沙盒。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://web.cs.ucdavis.edu/~su/teaching/ecs240-w17/readings/PLHistoryGoodDesign.PDF" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=web.cs.ucdavis.edu&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>web.cs.ucdavis.edu</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">104</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://web.cs.ucdavis.edu/~su/teaching/ecs240-w17/readings/PLHistoryGoodDesign.PDF" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>On the Design of Programming Languages (1974) [pdf]</span><span class='zh'>编程语言设计论（1974）</span></a><span class="dom">web.cs.ucdavis.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A landmark 1974 treatise explores core design decisions in programming languages, balancing expressiveness, simplicity, and practicality.</span><span class='zh'>这篇1974年的里程碑式论文探讨了编程语言设计中的核心取舍，强调表达能力、简洁性与实用性的平衡。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A landmark 1974 treatise explores core design decisions in programming languages, balancing expressiveness, simplicity, and practicality. It offers timeless observations on language abstractions, syntax, and semantics that still influence modern language design.</span><span class='zh'>这篇1974年的里程碑式论文探讨了编程语言设计中的核心取舍，强调表达能力、简洁性与实用性的平衡。它提出的关于语言抽象、语法与语义的见解对现代语言设计仍具影响力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://metaduck.com/reverse-engineering-the-wetware-spiking-networks-td-errors-and-the-end-of-matrix-math/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Reverse-Engineering the Wetware: Spiking Networks and the End of Matrix Math</span><span class='zh'>反向工程湿件：脉冲网络与矩阵数学的终结</span></a><span class="dom">metaduck.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Explores how spiking neural networks and TD errors offer alternative mathematical frameworks to traditional matrix-based models.</span><span class='zh'>讨论脉冲神经网络与 TD 误差如何提供超越传统矩阵数学的新框架，可能重塑 AI 理论与类脑硬件设计，推动神经形态计算的发展。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Explores how spiking neural networks and TD errors offer alternative mathematical frameworks to traditional matrix-based models. The article argues this could reshape AI theory and hardware design for neuromorphic computing.</span><span class='zh'>讨论脉冲神经网络与 TD 误差如何提供超越传统矩阵数学的新框架，可能重塑 AI 理论与类脑硬件设计，推动神经形态计算的发展。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arstechnica.com/gadgets/2026/03/amd-ryzen-ai-400-cpus-will-bring-upgraded-graphics-to-socket-am5-desktops/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AMD will bring its &quot;Ryzen AI&quot; processors to standard desktop PCs for first time</span><span class='zh'>AMD 将把 Ryzen AI 处理器首次带入主流桌面 PC</span></a><span class="dom">arstechnica.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AMD will bring Ryzen AI CPUs to mainstream AM5 desktop PCs for the first time, introducing AI acceleration and upgraded graphics to consumer hardware.</span><span class='zh'>AMD 将首次把 Ryzen AI 处理器带入主流 AM5 桌面电脑，集成 AI 加速和改进的显卡性能到消费级硬件中。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AMD will bring Ryzen AI CPUs to mainstream AM5 desktop PCs for the first time, introducing AI acceleration and upgraded graphics to consumer hardware. This move signals a broader industry push to bake AI capabilities into standard PCs, affecting software optimization and gaming workloads.</span><span class='zh'>AMD 将首次把 Ryzen AI 处理器带入主流 AM5 桌面电脑，集成 AI 加速和改进的显卡性能到消费级硬件中。这一举措标志着厂商在消费级电脑中普遍嵌入 AI 能力的趋势，它将影响软件优化和游戏等工作负载的性能表现。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Profiles a 1,700-year-old megastructure history that archaeologists nearly forgot, revealing new context about its construction and significance.</span><span class='zh'>聚焦一座被几近遗忘的1700年历史的巨型构筑物，揭示其建造方式与重要性的新线索，展示现代研究如何重新点燃对古代工程与旅行遗产的关注。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Profiles a 1,700-year-old megastructure history that archaeologists nearly forgot, revealing new context about its construction and significance. The story shows how modern research can revive interest in ancient engineering and travel heritage.</span><span class='zh'>聚焦一座被几近遗忘的1700年历史的巨型构筑物，揭示其建造方式与重要性的新线索，展示现代研究如何重新点燃对古代工程与旅行遗产的关注。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22874" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#10b981' data-tag='Science'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=arxiv.org&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>arxiv.org</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22874" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Flip Distance of Convex Triangulations and Tree Rotation Is NP-Complete</span><span class='zh'>凸三角剖分的翻转距离与树旋转问题 NP-完全</span></a><span class="dom">arxiv.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This arXiv paper proves that computing the flip distance between convex triangulations and related tree rotations is NP-complete.</span><span class='zh'>该论文证明计算凸三角剖分之间的翻转距离以及相关树旋转问题是 NP-完全问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This arXiv paper proves that computing the flip distance between convex triangulations and related tree rotations is NP-complete. The result sharpens our understanding of geometric transformations and has implications for algorithms in computational geometry.</span><span class='zh'>该论文证明计算凸三角剖分之间的翻转距离以及相关树旋转问题是 NP-完全问题。这一结果深化了对几何变换的理解，并对计算几何与相关算法研究具有重要意义。</span></p>
